\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}_{j}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}=\frac{W_{j}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}+\widetilde{R}_{0}.

(26)

Using Assumption
3
and conditions (i) – (iv),

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle|\widetilde{R}_{0}|\leq C\left(1+\frac{|W_{j}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right)\|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\|^{2}K_{\alpha,2}/\xi\ \ \mbox{for some absolute constant }C.\end{array}

The third inequality follows by applying the self-normalized moderate deviation theory. Let
\tilde{X}_{ij}:=W_{j}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})/\{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}\}
so under Assumptions
3
and
3
we have

\min_{j\in[p]}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{X}_{ij}^{2}]^{1/2}\geq\bar{\psi}_{2},\qquad\max_{j\in[p]}\mathbb{E}[|\tilde{X}_{ij}|^{3}]^{1/3}\leq\bar{K}_{3},\qquad\sqrt{\frac{\log^{3}p}{N}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{M^{3}}{N}}\leq\delta_{N},

so we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\tilde{X}_{ij}}{w_{j}}\right|>\frac{\Phi^{-1}(1-\delta/(2p))}{w_{j}N^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\tilde{X}_{ij}^{2}}\mbox{ for some }j\in[p]\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in[p]}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\tilde{X}_{ij}}{w_{j}}\right|>\frac{\Phi^{-1}(1-\delta/(2p))}{w_{j}N^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\tilde{X}_{ij}^{2}}\right)\leq\delta(1+\mathrm{o}(1)).\end{array}

where the first inequality follows from the union bound, and the second inequality follows by applying Theorem 2.3 of
Jing et al. (2003)
.

\blacksquare

Lemma EC.1.8

Suppose conditions (i) – (iv) of Lemma
EC.1.6
hold. Let
U:=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}}{(1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0})^{2}}
and

\hat{U}:=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}}{(1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0})^{2}}
.
Then
\left|U_{lk}-\hat{U}_{lk}\right|\leq 5\|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\|^{2}\frac{K_{\alpha,2}}{\xi}\max_{l,k\in[p]}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(1+|W_{il}|)(1+|W_{ik}|)
, where
W_{i}:=W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})/(1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0})
.

Proof EC.1.9

Proof of Lemma
EC.1.8

Note that by (
26
) we have
\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}=\frac{W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}+\widetilde{R}(\hat{\alpha},\alpha_{0},Y_{i})
,
where
|\widetilde{R}_{j}(\hat{\alpha},\alpha_{0},Y_{i})|\leq 2\left(1+\frac{|W_{j}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right)\|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\|^{2}K_{\alpha,2}/\xi
, for any
j\in[p]
. Define

\hat{W}_{i}:=\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}
,
\tilde{R}_{il}:=\tilde{R}_{l}(\hat{\alpha},\alpha_{0},Y_{i})
. Thus

\left|\hat{W}_{il}\hat{W}_{ik}-W_{il}W_{ik}\right|\leq\left|W_{il}\tilde{R}_{ik}+\tilde{R}_{il}W_{ik}+\tilde{R}_{il}\tilde{R}_{ik}\right|
. By (iv) of Lemma
EC.1.6
,
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}|\tilde{R}_{ik}|\left(1+\frac{|W_{l}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right)2\|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\|^{2}\frac{K_{\alpha,2}}{\xi}\leq\frac{1}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}|\tilde{R}_{ik}|\big(1+\frac{|W_{l}(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\big)
, so
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}|\tilde{R}_{il}\tilde{R}_{ik}|\leq\frac{1}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(1+|W_{il}|)|\tilde{R}_{ik}|
. So the upper bound for
\left|U_{lk}-\hat{U}_{lk}\right|
holds by the upper bound of
\tilde{R}_{il}
for any
l\in[p]
.

\blacksquare

Lemma EC.1.10

If
\displaystyle\left\lVert{\rm diag}^{-1}(w)\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{\lambda}{c}
for some constant
c>1
, then for
\nu=\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}
,
T
as the support of
r_{0}
, we have
\displaystyle\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\leq\frac{c+1}{c-1}\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}
.

Proof EC.1.11

Proof of Lemma
EC.1.10

For a given
\lambda
,
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left[\log\left(1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right)-\log\left(1+W\left(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i}\right)^{\prime}r_{0}\right)\right]\geq\lambda\left(\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)
.
Let
\hat{Q}(\alpha,r)=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\log\left(1+W\left(\alpha,Y_{i}\right)^{\prime}r\right)
, we can write the above inequality as
\hat{Q}(\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})-\hat{Q}(\hat{\alpha},r_{0})\geq\lambda\left(\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)
,
so using concavity of
\hat{Q}
in parameter
r
, we have
\lambda\left(\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)\geq\hat{Q}\left(\hat{\alpha},r_{0}\right)-\hat{Q}\left(\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right)\geq\nabla_{r}\hat{Q}(\hat{\alpha},r_{0})^{T}(r_{0}-\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})\geq_{(a)}-\frac{\lambda}{c}\left\lVert\nu\right\rVert_{w,1}
,
where (a) follows from Hölder’s inequality. Note that
\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}=\left\lVert r_{0}+\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}+\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\geq\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}+\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}
, so
\lambda(\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1})\leq\lambda\left(\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)
. Thus
\lambda\left(\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)\geq-\frac{\lambda}{c}\left\lVert\nu\right\rVert_{w,1}=-\frac{\lambda}{c}\left(\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}+\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)
,
implying
\frac{c+1}{c-1}\left\lVert\nu_{T}\right\rVert_{w,1}\geq\left\lVert\nu_{T^{c}}\right\rVert_{w,1}
.

\blacksquare

Lemma EC.1.12

Suppose Assumptions
3
and
3
hold. Suppose
\left\lVert{\rm diag}^{-1}(w)\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{\lambda}{c}
for some
c>1
and
\min_{i\in[N]}1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}\geq\underline{c}\xi
for an absolute constant
\underline{c}
. Then we have
\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{(1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0})}\right\}^{2}\leq 2\lambda\frac{c+1}{c-1}\left\lVert\hat{r}_{T}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}
, where
c_{0}>0
is a universal constant.

Proof EC.1.13

Proof of Lemma
EC.1.12

Let
T=\mathrm{supp}(r_{0})
and
f(Y_{i},\alpha,r)=1+W(\alpha,Y_{i})^{\prime}r
.
By optimality of
\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}
, we have
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})-\lambda\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\geq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},r_{0})-\lambda\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}
. So
\lambda\left(\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})-\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},r_{0})
. Moreover,
\displaystyle\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})-\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},r_{0})=\log\left(1+\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right)
. Additionally, note that given any
\bar{y}>-1
, let
c^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2(1+\max\{\bar{y},0\})}
, then
\log(1+y)\leq y-c^{\prime}y^{2}
holds for
y\in(-1,\bar{y}]
. Let
\bar{F}
be a universal constant such that
\max_{i\in[N]}\frac{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}\hat{r}}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\leq\bar{F}
, set
c_{0}=\{2\max(1,\bar{F})\}^{-1}
. Hence we have
\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}})-\log f(Y_{i},\hat{\alpha},r_{0})\leq\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}-c_{0}\left\{\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\}^{2}
, where
c_{0}>0
is a universal constant.
Thus
\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}+\lambda\left(\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\right)
. Since
\left\lVert{\rm diag}^{-1}(w)\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{\lambda}{c}
, by Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\left[W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0})\right]^{2}}{(1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0})^{2}}\\
&\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\frac{{\rm diag}^{-1}(w)}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})}{1+W(\hat{\alpha},Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}+\lambda\left\lVert r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}-\lambda\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1}\\
&\displaystyle\leq\lambda(1+1/c)\left\lVert\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}\leq\lambda(1+1/c)(\left\lVert\hat{r}_{T}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}+\left\lVert\hat{r}_{T^{c}}^{\mathrm{PI}}\right\rVert_{w,1})\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(a)}\lambda(1+1/c)\left\lVert\hat{r}_{T}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1}\left(1+\frac{c+1}{c-1}\right)=2\lambda\frac{c+1}{c-1}\left\lVert\hat{r}_{T}^{\mathrm{PI}}-r_{0}\right\rVert_{w,1},\end{array}

where inequality (a) follows from Lemma
EC.1.10
. Hence the result follows.

\blacksquare

Appendix EC.2

Additional Definitions and Proofs for Section
5.2

\hat{r}^{\mathrm{PI}}(\cdot,x)\in\arg\max_{a,b}\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}\left(\mbox{$X_{i}-x$}\right)\log(1+W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{a,b}(X_{i},x))-\lambda_{x}\|r_{a,b}\|_{w_{x},h,1}.

(27)

\hat{r}^{A}(\cdot,x)\in\arg\max_{a,b}\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}\left(\mbox{$X_{i}-x$}\right)\log(1+W(\alpha(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{a,b}(X_{i},x))-\lambda_{x}\|r_{a,b}\|_{w_{x},h,1}.

(28)

We define
\beta_{N,x}\!\!:={\displaystyle\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in[p]\\
k\in[d]\end{subarray}}}\left\{\frac{1}{w_{xj}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})},\frac{1}{w_{xjk}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{ik}-x_{k})/h|}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}
. Similar to its no-covariate counterpart,
\underline{\lambda}^{\mathrm{FO}}(x)
in the below characterizes the lower bound on the penalty parameter
\lambda
for the first-order estimator in the with-covariate case:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\underline{\lambda}^{\mathrm{FO}}(x)&\displaystyle:=\frac{\Phi^{-1}(1-\delta/\{2p(1+d)\})}{(Nh^{d})^{1/2}}{\displaystyle\max_{j\in[p]}}\frac{1}{w_{xj}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)W_{j}^{2}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{(1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i}))^{2}}}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\Phi^{-1}(1-\delta/\{2p(1+d)\})}{(Nh^{d})^{1/2}}{\displaystyle\max_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}}\frac{1}{w_{xjk}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{2}\big(\frac{X_{ik}-x_{k}}{h}\big)^{2}}{(1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i}))^{2}}}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+(1+\beta_{N,x})K_{\alpha,2}\max_{\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert^{2}/\xi+h^{2}.\end{array}

(29)

Define
\underline{\delta}_{N,x}
that characterizes the lower bound on
\delta_{N}
in the with-covariate case and parallels its no-covariate counterpart in Theorem
5.3
:

\underline{\delta}_{N,x}:=\max\left\{\frac{{\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}}\|\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}s}{\xi\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}^{2}{\displaystyle\min_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}}\{w_{xjk}^{2},w_{xj}^{2}\}},\frac{\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|}{\displaystyle\xi\min_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}\{w_{xjk},w_{xj}\}}\right\}.

(30)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\overline{R}_{x}&\displaystyle:=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h\\
\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})\end{subarray}}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\log(M/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+sdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+s^{2}d^{2}h^{4}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h\\
\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})\end{subarray}}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}+sdh^{2}\left(\frac{Md}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}\right).\end{array}

(31)

Similar to before, whenever a result is stated under Assumption
3
for the with-covariate case, we directly invoke the corresponding technical conditions from Assumption
Appendix
(i) – (vi).

EC.2.1

Proof of Theorem
5.5

It is sufficient to verify each condition of Lemma
EC.2.1
to prove Theorem
5.5
.
Firstly, the conditions (i)-(iv) hold as follows:

1.

Condition (i): By Lemma
EC.2.7
, with probability
1-\delta
where
\delta\geq\max\{\exp(-\bar{C}_{0}\{\sqrt{\log M}\sqrt{Nh^{d}}+Nh^{d}\}),\log(M)/(\delta_{N}Nh^{d})\}
for some universal constant
C
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}C\sqrt{\frac{\log(M/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}\\
&\quad\displaystyle{-}C\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h,\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\!\!\|\alpha(X_{i})\!-\!\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}\!\!-Csdh^{2}\left(\frac{Md}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}\right).\end{array}

Thus with

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})=\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h,\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}C\sqrt{\frac{\log(M/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+C\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h,\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}+Csdh^{2}\left(\frac{Md}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}\right)
, the first term in (i) of Lemma
EC.2.1
holds. Denote
\widetilde{W}_{i}:=\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
and
T_{i}:=\mathrm{supp}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))
, where
r_{*x}(X_{i}):=r_{0}(x)+\nabla r_{0}(x)(X_{i}-x)
. By Assumption
3

|T_{i}|\leq 2s
. Let
r_{0j}(\cdot)
denote the
j
-th element of
r_{0}(\cdot)
and
r_{*xj}(\cdot)
denote the
j
-th element of
r_{*x}(\cdot)
. Since
r_{*xj}(X_{i})-r_{0j}(X_{i})=r_{0j}(x)+\nabla_{x}r_{0j}(x)(X_{i}-x)-r_{0j}(X_{i})
, we have

\left|r_{*xj}(X_{i})-r_{0j}(X_{i})\right|\leq\bar{K}_{x,2}dh^{2}\ \mbox{for any $j\in T_{i}$},

(32)

where we use the definition of
\bar{K}_{x,2}
in the (component wise) Taylor expansion, and
\bar{K}_{x,2}
is independent of
s,h,N,M,d
. Then with probability
1-\delta_{\alpha}
,
\frac{1}{N}\!\!\sum_{i=1}^{N}\!K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\!\left\{\!\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\leq_{(1)}C^{\prime}s^{2}\{\max_{i\in[N],j\in T_{i}}(r_{0j}(X_{i})-r_{*xj}(X_{i}))^{2}\}\leq_{(2)}Cs^{2}d^{2}h^{4}
, where (1) holds with probability
1-\delta_{\alpha}
by Assumption
Appendix
and sparsity condition in Assumption
3
, (2) holds by (
32
), and
C,C^{\prime}
are universal constants. Thus the second term in (i) of Lemma
EC.2.1
holds with
\mathcal{G}(x)=Cs^{2}d^{2}h^{4}
.

2.

Condition (ii): By Lemma
EC.2.3
, the choice of
\lambda_{x}
in Theorem
5.5
makes (ii) of Lemma
EC.2.1
holds.

3.

Condition (iii) of Lemma
EC.2.1
follows immediately from Lemma
EC.2.5
, which holds with probability
1-\delta-\frac{C(\bar{K}_{\max}/\bar{K}_{2})^{2}}{Nh^{d}}
for some universal constant
C
.

4.

Condition (iv): note that by Taylor expansion, for each
j\in[p],i\in[n]
there is
\tilde{\alpha}^{ji}
in the line segment between
\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})
and
\alpha_{0}(X_{i})
such that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad W^{\mathrm{FO}}_{j}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})=W_{j}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})+(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W_{j}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\\
&=W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})+(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})+(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W_{j}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}^{2}W_{j}(\tilde{\alpha}^{ji},Y_{i})(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))\\
&=W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})+(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})+R_{ij}^{num}(x,\hat{\alpha}),\end{array}

where
R_{ij}^{num}(x,\hat{\alpha})
above contains second order terms. Following similar proof steps as above, for
R_{i}^{dem}(x,\hat{\alpha})
containing second order terms, we have
\frac{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}=1-\frac{(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})+R_{i}^{dem}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
. Particularly, Taylor expansion implies that for some
\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})
,
\bar{\alpha}(X_{i})
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})&=W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})+(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}^{2}\{W(\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})\}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}\{W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})\}\\
&=(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}))^{\prime}\left[\nabla_{\alpha}\{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})\}+\nabla_{\alpha}^{2}\{W(\bar{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})\}(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i}))\right].\end{array}

Combining all these arguments above, we have
\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}_{j}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}=\frac{W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}+\widetilde{R}_{ij}(x,\hat{\alpha})
,
|\widetilde{R}_{ij}(x,\hat{\alpha})|\leq C^{\prime}(\|\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|+\|\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2})
for some universal constant
C^{\prime}
. Thus (iv) of Lemma
EC.2.1
holds with
\widetilde{R}_{i}=C^{\prime}(\|\alpha_{*x}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|+\|\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2})
.

Hence from the argument above and statement of Theorem
5.5
, conditions (i)-(iv) of Lemma
EC.2.1
hold with probability
1-\delta
. Next, the remaining conditions of Lemma
EC.2.1
hold with probability
1-\delta_{\alpha}
by Assumption
Appendix
. Thus Theorem
5.5
holds.

\blacksquare

EC.2.2

Proof of Corollary
5.6

Recall
\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})=\prod_{j=1}^{M}\left\{\alpha_{j}+\beta_{j}(X-x):\begin{array}[]{l}\alpha_{j}\in[\hat{\alpha}_{j}(x)-cv_{x}\hat{s}_{j,0}(x),\hat{\alpha}_{j}(x)+cv_{x}\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)],\\
\beta_{jk}\in[\tilde{\beta}_{jk}(x)-cv_{x}\hat{s}_{j,k}(x),\tilde{\beta}_{jk}(x)+cv_{x}\hat{s}_{j,k}(x)],k\in[d]\end{array}\right\}
,

cv_{x}:=(1-\delta_{\alpha})\mbox{-quantile of}\max_{j\in[M],\ell\in\{0\}\cup[d]}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\xi_{i}\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}}{\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)}\right|

\mbox{conditional on }\{Y_{i},X_{i}\}_{i\in[N]}
where
\{\xi_{i}\}_{i\in[N]}
are i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
random variables. Here
\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}:=Y_{ij}-\hat{\alpha}_{j}(x)-\tilde{\beta}_{j}(x)(X_{i}-x)
, and
\hat{\zeta}_{i}:=(Z^{\prime}W_{x}Z)^{-1}Z_{i}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)
with
Z_{i}=(1,(X_{i}-x)^{\prime})^{\prime}
and
W_{x}
is a diagonal
N\times N
matrix with
W_{x,ii}:=K_{h}(X_{i}-x)
,
\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x):=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}^{2}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}^{2}}
, where
\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}
is the
\ell
-th entry of
\hat{\zeta}_{i}
,
\forall\ell\in\{0\}\cup[d]
. Note that by triangular inequality we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\geq t\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\ +\mathbb{P}\left(cv_{x}\sqrt{\sum_{j\in[M]}\left(\sum_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)|X_{il}-x_{l}|+\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)\right)^{2}}\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(a)}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\ +\mathbb{P}\left(cv_{x}\sqrt{2dh^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{M}\max_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)^{2}+2\sum_{j=1}^{M}\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)^{2}}\geq\frac{t}{2}\right),\end{array}

where the first inequality follows from the triangular inequality, (a) above follows because
\sum_{\ell\in[d]}(X_{i\ell}-x_{\ell})^{2}\leq h^{2}
,
\sum_{j\in[M]}\bigg(\sum_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)(X_{il}-x_{l})+\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)\bigg)^{2}\leq 2dh^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{M}\max_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)^{2}+2\sum_{j=1}^{M}\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)^{2}
. Note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(1)}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert(\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x))+(\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x))(X_{i}-x)\right\rVert+\sqrt{M}\bar{K}_{x,2}\|X_{i}-x\|^{2}\geq\frac{t}{2}\right),\end{array}

where (1) holds by Taylor expansion and Assumption
Appendix
. Furthermore, Proposition
EC.3.2
implies that both
\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x)\right\rVert_{2}\leq\tilde{C}_{0}(x)\left(\sqrt{\frac{M}{Nh^{d}}}+\sqrt{M}dh^{2}+\sqrt{\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d}}+h^{4}d^{2}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right)
and
\left\lVert\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x)\right\rVert_{2,2}\leq\displaystyle\tilde{C}_{1}(x)\sqrt{d}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{M}{Nh^{d+2}}}+\sqrt{M}dh^{2}+\sqrt{\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d+2}}+h^{4}d^{4}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\bigg)
hold with probability
1-\delta
for some absolute constants
\tilde{C}_{0}(x)
and
\tilde{C}_{1}(x)
. Thus for
X_{i}
such that
\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h
, with probability
1-\delta
, for some absolute constant
\tilde{C}(x)
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert(\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x))+(\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x))(X_{i}-x)\right\rVert\\
&\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}(x)\left[\sqrt{\frac{M}{Nh^{d}}}+\sqrt{M}dh^{2}+\sqrt{\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d}}+h^{4}d^{2}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{C}(x)\left[h\sqrt{d}\left(\sqrt{\frac{M}{Nh^{d+2}}}+\sqrt{M}dh^{2}+\sqrt{\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d+2}}+h^{4}d^{4}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right)\right].\end{array}

Additionally,
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}^{2}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}^{2}}{\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)^{2}}=1
for any
j\in[M],\ell\in[d]\cup\{0\}
, so
conditional on
\{Y_{i},X_{i}\}
,
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\xi_{i}\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}}{\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
, thus

\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{j\in[M],\ell\in\{0\}\cup[d]}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\xi_{i}\frac{\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}}{\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)}\right]\leq\sqrt{2\log\{M(d+1)\}}
.
By Lemma
EC.1.3
(Borell-TIS inequality
(
Borell 1975
)
), with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\max_{j\in[M],\ell\in[d]\cup\{0\}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\xi_{i}\hat{\epsilon}_{ij}\hat{\zeta}_{i\ell}}{\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)}\right|\leq 2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{M(d+1)}{\delta}\right)}.

(33)

Let
\delta\leq\delta_{\alpha}
and recall that
\delta\geq\exp(-C\{\sqrt{\log M}\sqrt{Nh^{d}}+Nh^{d}\})
, and set
t=C\sqrt{\frac{Md\log\{M(d+1)/\delta\}}{Nh^{d}}}+C\sqrt{M}dh^{2}+C\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}
for some absolute constant
C
in the above, then the above results imply that
\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\leq C\sqrt{Md}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log\{M(d+1)/\delta\}}{Nh^{d}}}+\sqrt{d}h^{2}\right]+C\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}
holds with probability
1-2\delta
. Additionally, by triangular inequality, for any
t^{\prime}>0
we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\geq t^{\prime}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\!\!\geq\!\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\left(\!cv_{x}\!\!\left[\sum_{j\in[M],\ell\in[d]}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!|\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)(X_{i\ell}-x_{\ell})|+\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{j\in[M]}\!\!\!\!|\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)|\right]\geq\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(b)}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\geq\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathbb{P}\left(cv_{x}\left[\sqrt{Md}h\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{M}\max_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}^{2}(x)}+\sqrt{M}\sqrt{\sum_{j\in[M]}\hat{s}_{j,0}^{2}(x)}\right]\geq\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right),\end{array}

where (b) follows since

\sum_{j\in[M],\ell\in[d]}|\hat{s}_{j,\ell}(x)(X_{il}-x_{l})|+\sum_{j\in[M]}|\hat{s}_{j,0}(x)|\leq\sqrt{Md}h\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{M}\max_{\ell\in[d]}\hat{s}_{j,\ell}^{2}(x)}+\sqrt{M}\sqrt{\sum_{j\in[M]}\hat{s}_{j,0}^{2}(x)}.

Note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)+\tilde{\beta}(x)(X_{i}-x)-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\geq\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(1)}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert(\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x))+(\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x))(X_{i}-x)\right\rVert_{1}+M\bar{K}_{x,2}h^{2}\geq\frac{t^{\prime}}{2}\right),\end{array}

where (1) holds by Taylor expansion. By Proposition
EC.3.2
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x)\right\rVert_{1}\leq\tilde{C}_{0}(x)\left(\frac{M}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}+\sqrt{M\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d}}+h^{4}d^{2}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right),

and
\left\lVert\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x)\right\rVert_{1,1}\leq\tilde{C}_{1}(x)d\left(\frac{M}{\sqrt{Nh^{d+2}}}+Md^{2}h^{2}+\sqrt{M\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d+2}}+h^{4}d^{4}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right)
.
Henceforth, for some absolute constant
\tilde{C}^{\prime}(x)
, for
X_{i}
such that
\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, it holds that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert(\hat{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x))+(\tilde{\beta}(x)-\nabla\alpha_{0}(x))(X_{i}-x)\right\rVert_{1}\\
&\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}^{\prime}(x)\left[\frac{M}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}+\sqrt{M\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d}}+h^{4}d^{2}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{C}^{\prime}(x)\left[hd\left(\frac{M}{\sqrt{Nh^{d+2}}}+Md^{2}h^{2}+\sqrt{M\left(\frac{1}{Nh^{d+2}}+h^{4}d^{4}\right)\log(1/\delta)}\right)\right].\end{array}

Let
\delta\leq\delta_{\alpha}
, set
t^{\prime}=C^{\prime}\frac{Md}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+C^{\prime}Mdh^{2}+C^{\prime}d\sqrt{\frac{M\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}
in the above, where
C^{\prime}
is an absolute constant. Note that Assumption
3
implies
dh^{2}<1
, then the previous results imply that with probability
1-2\delta
,
\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\leq C^{\prime}M\left(\frac{d}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+dh^{2}\right)+C^{\prime}d\sqrt{\frac{M\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}
. Note that
\lambda_{x}=C\sqrt{\log(p/\delta)/Nh^{d}}+Ch^{2}
under the given condition, Theorem
5.5
implies that with probability
1-C\delta-C\delta_{\alpha}-\frac{C(\bar{K}_{\max}/\bar{K}_{2})^{2}}{Nh^{d}}-C\epsilon
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(\hat{\alpha})&\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\log(M/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+C_{1}sdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+C_{1}s^{2}d^{2}h^{4}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}\!\!\!\!\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}+C_{1}sdh^{2}\left(\frac{Md}{\sqrt{Nh^{d}}}+Mdh^{2}\right)\leq_{(1)}C_{2}(M+s^{2})d^{2}h^{4},\end{array}

where
C,C_{1},C_{2}
are constants independent of
s,N,M,h,d
above. Here (1) above uses Assumption
3
, and the high-probability bounds derived for
\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}
and
\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\|\alpha(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}
. Thus, Theorem
5.5
implies
\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left(\Delta_{x,i}^{\mathrm{FO}}\right)^{2}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{sM+s\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+C(\sqrt{M}+s)dh^{2}
. Further, Assumption
3
requires
h
to satisfy

\left(\frac{M^{3}}{N\delta_{N}^{2}}\right)^{1/d}\leq h\leq\sqrt{\frac{\delta_{N}\xi}{sd}}
. By first order condition, we have
h^{*}=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\delta_{N}\xi}{sd}},\max\left\{\left(\frac{M^{3}}{N\delta_{N}^{2}}\right)^{1/d},4^{-\frac{2}{d+4}}\left(\frac{sM+s\log(1/\delta)}{N(s+\sqrt{M})^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{d+4}}\right\}\right\}
. Particularly, when
h=h^{*}
, by direct calculation, we have
\sqrt{\frac{sM+s\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+(\sqrt{M}+s)dh^{2}\leq\bar{C}\min\left\{B_{1},B_{2}\right\}
for some universal constant
\bar{C}
, where
B_{1}:=\delta_{N}\sqrt{\frac{s(M+\log(1/\delta))}{M^{3}}}+(\sqrt{M}+s)d\left(\frac{M^{3}}{N\delta_{N}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2}{d}}
,

B_{2}:=\sqrt{\frac{s(M+\log(1/\delta))}{N}}\left(\frac{sd}{\delta_{N}\xi}\right)^{d/4}\!\!+\frac{(\sqrt{M}+s)\delta_{N}\xi}{s}
.

\blacksquare

EC.2.3

Technical Lemmas for Section
5.2

Lemma EC.2.1

For any
x\in\mathcal{X}
, define
\alpha_{*x}(X):=\alpha_{0}(x)+\nabla_{x}\alpha_{0}(x)(X-x)
and define
r_{*x}(X):=r_{0}(x)+\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)(X-x)
. Moreover for any
\bar{\alpha}\in[0,1]^{M},\bar{y}\in\{0,1\}^{M}
, let
W^{\mathrm{FO}}_{i}(\bar{\alpha},\bar{y})=W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),\bar{y})+\nabla_{\alpha}W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),\bar{y})(\bar{\alpha}-\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}))
. Suppose (i)
\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})\geq-\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})
, and
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\leq\mathcal{G}(x)
,

(ii)
\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\geq\left\|\mathrm{diag}^{-1}(w_{x})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\begin{pmatrix}W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\\
{\rm vec}(W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\frac{(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}}{h})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}

(iii)
\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right|\leq Csdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}

(iv)
\displaystyle\left\|\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}-\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{R}_{i}
. (v)
\alpha_{*x}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})
.

(vi)
\displaystyle\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h,j\in[p],\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}|v^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W_{j}(\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})|+|v^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W(\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})|\leq M^{1/2}K_{\alpha,1}\|v\|
,

(vii)
\displaystyle\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h,j\in[p],\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}|u^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}^{2}W(\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})v|+|u^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}^{2}W_{j}(\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})v|\leq K_{\alpha,2}\|u\|\|v\|
, and

{\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{x}\in\mathcal{X},i\in[N]}}|u^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W(\alpha_{0}(\tilde{x}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})|\leq\bar{K}_{w}\|u\|_{1}
,
{\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{x}\in\mathcal{X},i\in[N]}}|u^{\prime}\nabla_{\alpha}W(\alpha_{0}(\tilde{x}),Y_{i})^{\prime}v|\leq\bar{K}_{w}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{\infty}\left\lVert v\right\rVert_{1}
. (viii)
{\displaystyle\max_{\|\tilde{x}-x\|\leq h,\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}}\|\alpha(x)-\alpha_{0}(x)\|M^{1/2}K_{\alpha,1}/\xi<1/4
,
{\displaystyle\max_{\|\tilde{x}-x\|\leq h,\tilde{\alpha}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}}\|\tilde{\alpha}(x)-\alpha_{0}(x)\|^{2}K_{\alpha,2}/\xi\leq 1/16
.
(ix)
{\displaystyle\max_{j\in[p],k\in[M],x\in\mathcal{X}}}|v^{\prime}\nabla_{x}^{2}r_{0j}(x)v|+|v^{\prime}\nabla^{2}\alpha_{0k}(x)v|\leq\bar{K}_{x,2}\|v\|^{2}
.

(x)
\delta_{N}\geq s{\displaystyle\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}}\|\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}/\{\xi\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}^{2}{\displaystyle\min_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}}\{w_{xjk}^{2},w_{xj}^{2}\}\}
.

\delta_{N}\geq{\displaystyle\max_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}}\|\tilde{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|/\{\xi{\displaystyle\min_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}}\{w_{xjk},w_{xj}\}\}
.

Then for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
, for some universal constant
C
, with probability
1-C\epsilon
,

\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}}\leq C\left(\lambda_{x}\left(1+\frac{1}{c}\right)\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x)^{1/2}\right),

where
\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x)\leq\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})+\mathcal{G}(x)+Csdh^{2}\left(\!\!\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}\!+\!\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|\widetilde{R}_{i}|\!+\!\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|\widetilde{R}_{i}|^{2}\right)
.

Proof EC.2.2

Proof of Lemma
EC.2.1

Let
Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{a,b}):=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\log(1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{a,b}(X_{i},x))
. Following similar proof steps as in Proposition
5.2
and Theorem
5.3
, using Assumption
3
and the given conditions, with probability
1-C\epsilon
, we have
1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i}))>c_{1}\xi,1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})>c_{1}\xi
for some
c_{1}\in(0,1)
. Additionally, note that given any
\bar{y}>-1
, there exists a constant
c^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2(1+\max\{\bar{y},0\})}
such that
\log(1+y)\leq y-c^{\prime}y^{2}
holds for
y\in(-1,\bar{y}]
. Hence for some universal constant
c_{0}>0
we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\log(1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i}))-\log(1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i}))\\
&\displaystyle=\log\left(1+\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right)\\
&\displaystyle\leq\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}-c_{0}\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2},\end{array}

Define

\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}:=\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})},\qquad\mbox{then}

Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{0})\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}-c_{0}(\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}})^{2}\right\}.

(34)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mbox{Next note that}&\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\\
&\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{a}-r_{0}(x)+\hat{b}(X_{i}-x)-\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)(X_{i}-x))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}}_{\textrm{(I)}}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}}_{\textrm{(II)}}=\textrm{(I)}+\textrm{(II)}.\ \ \mbox{Note that}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}|\textrm{(I)}|&\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathrm{diag}^{-1}(w_{x})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\left({W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\frac{(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}}{h})}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left\|\left({\hat{a}-r_{0}(x)}\atop{{\rm vec}\big(h(\hat{b}-\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)})\big)\right)\right\|_{w_{x},1}\leq\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\left\|\left({\hat{a}-r_{0}(x)}\atop{{\rm vec}\big(h(\hat{b}-\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)})\big)\right)\right\|_{w_{x},1},\end{array}

where in the above, the first inequality holds by Hölder’s inequality and the second inequality holds by condition (ii). Since
r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i})=r_{0}(x)+\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)(X_{i}-x)-r_{0}(X_{i})
, by Taylor expansion,
\left\|r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i})\right\|_{1}=\|r_{0}(x)+\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)(X_{i}-x)-r_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}\leq\bar{K}sdh^{2}
for some universal constant
\bar{K}
by condition (ix). Define
\Delta^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},Y_{i}):=\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}-\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}
.
So for some universal constant
C
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}|\textrm{(II)}|&\displaystyle\leq_{(1)}\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right|\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\Delta^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},Y_{i})(r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i}))\bigg|\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(2)}Csdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+\frac{1}{N}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\|\Delta^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},Y_{i})\right\|_{\infty}\|r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i})\|_{1}\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(3)}Csdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+\frac{Csdh^{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i},\end{array}

where (1) holds by the triangle inequality, the first term of (2) holds by condition (iii), and the second term of (2) holds by Hölder’s inequality, (3) holds by condition (iv) and the bound for
\left\|r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i})\right\|_{1}
above. So the arguments above imply

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}\\
&\displaystyle\leq Csdh^{2}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}\right]+\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\left\|\left({\hat{a}-r_{0}(x)}\atop{{\rm vec}\big(h(\hat{b}-\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)})\big)\right)\right\|_{w_{x},1}.\end{array}

(35)

Let
a^{*}:=r_{0}(x),b^{*}:=\nabla r_{0}(x)
. So
r_{*x}(X_{i})=r_{a^{*},b^{*}}(X_{i},x)
, and

\left\|\left({\hat{a}-r_{0}(x)}\atop{{\rm vec}\big(h(\hat{b}-\nabla_{x}r_{0}(x)})\big)\right)\right\|_{w_{x},1}=\big\|\big(\hat{a}-a^{*},{\rm vec}\big(h(\hat{b}-b^{*})\big)\big)\big\|_{w_{x},1}=\|r_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}.

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})-\lambda_{x}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\\
&\geq_{(1)}\displaystyle\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})-\lambda_{x}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\geq_{(2)}\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{*x})-\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\\
&\displaystyle\geq\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\{Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})\}+Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})-\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\\
&\displaystyle\geq_{(3)}-\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})+Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})-\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\end{array}

(36)

where (1) holds from condition (v)
\alpha_{*x}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})
, (2) holds by the optimality of
\hat{a},\hat{b}
, (3) holds since
\min_{\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})}\{Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha,r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})\}\geq-\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})
by condition (i). Hence,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)(\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}})^{2}\leq_{(1)}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)(\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}})-\{Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{0})\}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)(\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}})-\{Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})+Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{0})\}\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(2)}2Csdh^{2}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}\right]+\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\\
&\quad\quad\displaystyle+\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}-\lambda_{x}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha}),\ \mbox{where (1) is due to }\eqref{eq:covariate:quadratic:Q},\mbox{(2) from \eqref{eq:bound:I:II}, \eqref{eq:covariate:main:steps}}\end{array}

and following from similar proof steps for (
34
),
-(Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{*x})-Q^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x},r_{0}))\leq|\textrm{(II)}|
. The above inequality is equivalent to
\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\leq\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}-\lambda_{x}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})+\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+2Csdh^{2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}\right)
. Define
\Delta_{*x,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}:=\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
,
\Delta_{0}W_{i}:=\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
.
Then for some universal constant
C^{\prime}
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{0,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}\right\}^{2}}-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{*x,ab}^{\mathrm{FO}}\right\}^{2}}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},Y_{i})(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))\right\}^{2}}-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{0}W_{i}\right\}^{2}}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\|\Delta^{\mathrm{FO}}(X_{i},Y_{i})\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\|r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i})\|^{2}_{1}}-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{0}W_{i}\right\}^{2}}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-C^{\prime}sdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}^{2}}-\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\Delta_{0}W_{i}\right\}^{2}},\end{array}

where the last inequality follows from condition (iv) and the bound for
\left\|r_{*x}(X_{i})-r_{0}(X_{i})\right\|_{1}
above. Using the fact that that for non-negative numbers
a,b,c
we have
a+c\geq b\Rightarrow 2a^{2}+2c^{2}\geq b^{2}
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\frac{2c_{0}}{N}\!\!\sum_{i=1}^{N}\!K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\!\!\left\{\!\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\!\!\!\!\!+\!\!4\!\left\{\!C^{\prime}sdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}^{2}}\right\}^{2}\\
&\displaystyle+\frac{4c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\\
&\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}.\ \mbox{Next, define }\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})&\displaystyle:=\mathcal{R}_{x}(\hat{\alpha})+2Csdh^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{Nh^{d}}}+2(C^{\prime})^{2}s^{2}d^{2}h^{4}\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}^{2}\\
&\displaystyle\ +\frac{2Csdh^{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\widetilde{R}_{i}+\frac{2c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(r_{0}(X_{i})-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}.\end{array}

The upper bound for
\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{0}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}
then further implies that
\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}\leq\lambda_{x}\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}-\lambda_{x}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+\frac{\lambda_{x}}{c}\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
. Define
\displaystyle D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}:=\left\{\frac{W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}
. Let
T_{x}
denote the support of
r_{a^{*},b^{*}}
. Using the fact that
\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}-\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq\|r_{a^{*},b^{*}}-(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}
,

\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})\leq\lambda_{x}(1+1/c)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}-\lambda_{x}(1-1/c)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}

(37)

Let
\mathcal{W}_{x}:={\rm diag}(w_{x})
. Define
U_{x},\hat{U}_{x}
as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}U_{x}&:=&\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\displaystyle\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left({W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}/h)}\right)\left({W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}/h)}\right)^{\prime}}{(1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i}))^{2}},\\
\hat{U}_{x}&:=&\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\left({W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}/h)}\right)\left({W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}/h)}\right)^{\prime}}{(1+W^{\mathrm{FO}}(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i}))^{2}}.\end{array}

Let
\hat{v}_{x}=[(\hat{a})^{\prime},h{\rm vec}(\hat{b})^{\prime}]^{\prime}-[(a^{*})^{\prime},h{\rm vec}(b^{*})^{\prime}]^{\prime}
. By Hölder’s inequality,

\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}-\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}\geq\!-\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\!\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\!(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\!\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}

(38)

We next consider two exclusive cases:
\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\geq 2\frac{c+1}{c-1}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}
and
\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}<2\frac{c+1}{c-1}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}
. Firstly suppose
Case 1
:
\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\geq 2\frac{c+1}{c-1}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}
.
then (
37
) implies
\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{x}\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T^{c}_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
, which immediately implies
\lambda_{x}\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T^{c}_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq 2\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
. Then

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}-\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}\geq-\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\left(1+\frac{(c-1)}{2(c+1)}\right)^{2}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\left(1+\frac{(c-1)}{2(c+1)}\right)^{2}\ \frac{4\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{2}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{(1-\frac{1}{c})^{2}\lambda^{2}_{x}}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\geq-\frac{9\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{2}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{(1-1/c)^{2}\lambda_{x}^{2}}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}.\end{array}

Rearranging the terms implies
\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}\leq\frac{9\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{2}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{(1-1/c)^{2}\lambda_{x}^{2}}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(\hat{U}_{x}-U_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}+\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}
, which implies
\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}\leq\frac{9\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{2}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{(1-1/c)^{2}\lambda_{x}^{2}}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(\hat{U}_{x}-U_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
. This inequality holds since (
37
) implies that
\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{x}\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T^{c}_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
, which immediately implies
\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}=\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
. Thus in Case 1 we have
\sqrt{\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}}\leq\frac{3\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})}{(1-1/c)\lambda_{x}}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(\hat{U}_{x}-U_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}^{1/2}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})^{1/2}
. By Taylor expansion,
\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(\hat{U}_{x}-U_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}\leq C_{1}(\max_{i\in[N]}\|\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\|^{2}+h^{2})
for some universal constant
C_{1}
from the conditions (vi) – (viii).
Condition (x) further implies that
\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(\hat{U}_{x}-U_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}^{1/2}
is negligible for
N
sufficiently large. Hence for some universal constant
C_{2}
, under case 1 we have

\sqrt{\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}}\leq C_{2}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})^{1/2}.

(39)

Next suppose
Case 2
holds:
\displaystyle\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq 2\frac{c+1}{c-1}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}
. Define

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{ab}^{i}:=\left\{\frac{W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}(X_{i},x)-r_{*x}(X_{i}))}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\right\}^{2}.

Then we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}=\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\hat{\Delta}_{ab}^{i}\leq_{(1)}\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}+\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\!\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(2)}\lambda_{x}(1+\frac{1}{c})\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}+\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\!\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(3)}\lambda_{x}(1+1/c)\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+2\left[4\left(\frac{c+1}{c-1}\right)^{2}+1\right]\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(4)}\frac{\lambda_{x}}{\sqrt{c_{0}}}\left(1+\frac{1}{c}\right)\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}}\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\hat{\Delta}_{ab}^{i}}+\frac{s\eta_{0}\|\!\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\!(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\!\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\!\|_{\infty,\infty}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}^{2}}\!\!\!\sum_{i=1}^{N}\!\!\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)\hat{\Delta}_{ab}^{i}}{N}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha}),\end{array}

(40)

where (1) of (
40
) follows from (
38
), (2) of (
40
) follows from (
37
) and the fact that
\hat{v}_{x}\hat{U}_{x}\hat{v}_{x}=\frac{c_{0}}{2N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{h}(X_{i}-x)D_{i*}^{\mathrm{FO}}
, (3) follows since
\|\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\leq 2\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}+2\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}})_{T_{x}^{c}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}\leq 2\left[4(\frac{c+1}{c-1})^{2}+1\right]\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}^{2}
,
(4) follows from the definition of restricted eigenvalue and the fact that
\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,1}\leq\sqrt{s}\|(\hat{r}_{\hat{a},\hat{b}}-r_{a^{*},b^{*}})_{T_{x}}\|_{w_{x},h,2}
, with
\eta_{0}=2\left[4\left(\frac{c+1}{c-1}\right)^{2}+1\right]
.
Note that by condition (x),
\frac{s\eta_{0}\|\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}(U_{x}-\hat{U}_{x})\mathcal{W}_{x}^{-1}\|_{\infty,\infty}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}^{2}}\leq c_{0}/4
when
N
is sufficiently large. Further note that
\frac{1}{4}t^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{x}}{\sqrt{c_{0}}}(1+\frac{1}{c})\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}}t+\mathcal{\widetilde{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})\Rightarrow t\leq\frac{4}{\sqrt{c_{0}}}\lambda_{x}(1+\frac{1}{c})\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}}+2{\mathcal{\widetilde{R}}}^{1/2}(x,\hat{\alpha})
. Let
t=\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}}
, then we have
\sqrt{\hat{v}_{x}^{\prime}U_{x}\hat{v}_{x}}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{c_{0}}}\lambda_{x}(1+\frac{1}{c})\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\kappa_{x,\bar{c}}}+\sqrt{2}{\mathcal{\widetilde{R}}}^{1/2}(x,\hat{\alpha})
in Case 2. Recall that (
39
) holds in Case 1. Thus the result holds from the definition of
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(x,\hat{\alpha})
and the second statement of condition (i).

\blacksquare

Recall from Section
5.2
that
\beta_{N,x}:={\displaystyle\max_{j\in[p],k\in[d]}}\left\{\mathcal{B}_{1,xj},\mathcal{B}_{2,xjk}\right\}
, where for any

,
\mathcal{B}_{1,xj}:=\frac{1}{w_{xj}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})|}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
,
\mathcal{B}_{2,xjk}:=\frac{1}{w_{xjk}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)|W_{j}(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})(X_{ik}-x_{k})/h|}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}
.

Lemma EC.2.3

Under Assumptions
3
,
3
,
5.2
,
Appendix
, for some absolute constant
C
,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\left\|\mathrm{diag}^{-1}(w_{x})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)}{1+W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\left({W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W(\hat{\alpha}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\frac{(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}}{h})}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathrm{diag}^{-1}(w_{x})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\left({W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\frac{(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}}{h})}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+C(1+\beta_{N,x})\max_{\|X_{i}-x\|\leq h}\left\lVert\hat{\alpha}(X_{i})-\alpha_{0}(X_{i})\right\rVert\frac{M^{1/2}K_{\alpha,1}}{\xi},\end{array}

&\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathrm{diag}^{-1}(w_{x})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{K_{h}(X_{i}-x)}{1+W(\alpha_{0}(X_{i}),Y_{i})^{\prime}r_{0}(X_{i})}\left({W(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})}\atop{{\rm vec}(W(\alpha_{*x}(X_{i}),Y_{i})\frac{(X_{i}-x)^{\prime}}{h})}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\\
